### AV-0198 — Eight complete Event text/native/still cases with unheard audio

B0251 covers eight complete Event documents in three original families of 2, 2 and 4 chapters, catalog 892–899: 632 native pages, 587 written TextAreas, 45 command-only pages, 479 dialogue-voice pages and 4,326 visual references. All 101 bounded text/native parts and three separate whole-family reconstructions were completed in the preserved order. The visual review covers 232 selected sprite/object instances across 119 distinct resources, 13 background instances across nine originals, and six title-image instances across four files, with genuine exact-byte reuse disclosed. All 490 available, distinct WAV bindings remain unheard. That page has no TextArea, and all dialogue cues resolve. No Choice or InlineMovie occurs. | `CL-B0252-001` | Identification preserves the right to deny autobiography `THEMATIC_INTERPRETATION`. | The scene gives four performers the position of an audience. They classify, remember, admire and dispute the work in front of them. That change of position makes reception an event in its own right: Musubi can identify with a heartbreak situation while refusing Susu's inference that it discloses her own history. The correction is part of the scene's meaning, not noise to remove when extracting a trait. P47-58 preserves imaginative competence without forcing confession. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0221.json__24c714f73fdfc6f0?doc=primary&log=47#/Pages/47` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0221.json__24c714f73fdfc6f0?doc=primary&log=58#/Pages/58` (complete P47-58 context). All 95 bounded text/native parts and 170 selected sprite/object instances, 18 original background instances and six title instances were actually reviewed. ## B0253 assumed illness, care and uneven knowledge: interpretations

Owner [B0253](../02%20Readings/T7S_B0253_DEEP_READING.md), reading SHA256 `14aa686625e7795f75c1ac5d85b24d4b71cf248e8f69616b847fd7f0aa9ea7e7`; SourceLock 12.162, witness T7S_GAME_OFFLINE_JA_R484, corpus c20260909-r484. Exact scope and limits: bound reading and AV-0200.

| ID | Claim and kind | Argument, evidence and retained horizon |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `CL-B0253-001` | The opening converts a disturbance of familiar sociability into a miniature epidemic narrative. `THEMATIC_INTERPRETATION` | The opening converts a disturbance of familiar sociability into a miniature epidemic narrative. Warmth, tea and Tomoe's naming of everyday peace create the baseline; Kajika's hearsay introduces exceptional danger, and three short encounters are then read through that frame. The causal sequence actually established is rumor → attention to changed conduct → classification → proposed investigation, not pathogen → infection → symptoms. Yumeno's explanation gives a name to observations without supplying a demonstrated cause. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23` (complete P0–23 context). D0 for written/native acts;D1 for bounded interpretation. Complete source/known-then limits,alternatives and counterevidence remain in the owner reading;no performed voice or readiness promotion. |
| `CL-B0253-002` | Player's care is sincere enough to generate inquiry, but his measure of Makoto's welfare is comic and ethically unstable. `THEMATIC_INTERPRETATION` | Player's care is sincere enough to generate inquiry, but his measure of Makoto's welfare is comic and ethically unstable. Makoto's ordinary departure becomes alarming because intense attention and even appearing in a locker are his reported norm. Tomoe's objection at P35 prevents the reading from adopting Player's baseline without criticism. Earlier records of Makoto's affectionate care and privacy costs remain differentiated: this chapter neither erases that care nor endorses intrusion as the standard by which health should be judged. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=47#/Pages/47` (complete P24–47 context). | `CL-B0253-003` | The visitors form an escalation rather than three interchangeable diagnostic examples. `THEMATIC_INTERPRETATION` | The visitors form an escalation rather than three interchangeable diagnostic examples. Makoto gives a comparatively ordinary denial and lesson plan; Susu directly refuses the illness premise and becomes terse; Saori substitutes an elaborate fantasy of dangerous power. Player's repeated perplexity links them, while Tomoe and Kajika receive Saori's claim differently. The variations matter because the chapter has not established that the three share a motive, a real illness, involuntary loss of control or the same degree of participation. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=70#/Pages/70` (complete P24–70 context). She first questions whether Player should worry, then recognizes an embarrassing resemblance and supplies her own dark-wizard title. Her offer to accompany the inquiry turns self-exposure into a possible resource for helping others. This is local willingness grounded in remembered experience; it is neither a reversal of her separately bounded leadership history nor proof of permanent confidence, medical recovery or an exact age/date. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36` (complete P32–36 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=61#/Pages/61` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0224.json__db62f80a92180f37?doc=primary&log=71#/Pages/71` (complete P61–71 context).
